# P3 build conventions

Derived from the frozen spec (S01, S16; build order S19.5) at packet P3-B0-1, 2026-07-19. Every packet session follows this file; on any conflict the spec wins and the conflict is reported to the coordinator. The file is append-mostly: later packets add the conventions their sections ground (each addition cites its spec section) and never silently rewrite earlier ones.

## 1. Repository layout (S01.5, S16.2, S19.5)

```
cmd/sinet/            the one release binary — mode dispatch only
internal/             all platform packages (module-private; nothing is importable from outside)
tools/                repo/CI tooling (e.g. tools/lockgate) — never part of the release artifact
.github/workflows/    CI (S01.11)
components.lock       adoption manifest (S16.2) — repo root
P3/                   build coordination (STATE.md is coordinator-owned; packets never edit it)
Spec/  Research/  Docs/   read-only for build sessions
```

- **One binary.** The release artifact is a single static Go binary, `sinet`; `sinet-control`, `sinet-broker`, and `sinet-portpool` are the same binary invoked in dedicated modes (S01.5 multi-call posture). Never add a second `cmd/` — a new daemon is a new mode plus a unit file, not a new artifact.
- Daemon modes live in the mode table in `internal/cli`; modes not yet built are reserved names that fail with "not implemented in this build" (exit 1) so invocation shape and future `ExecStart=` lines are stable from the first build.
- SPA assets embed into this same binary via `go:embed` at B6 (S01.5); nothing about that lands earlier.
- No `pkg/` directory: this module is never imported, so everything beyond `cmd/` and `tools/` is `internal/`.

## 2. Go conventions (S01.5)

- **Module path:** `github.com/dariannixda-eng/Sinet-Agentic-Control-Hub` (the real repo; private, never fetched by proxy — the uppercase path is accepted).
- **Toolchain pin:** the `go` directive in `go.mod` (`1.26.5`) is the single source of truth; the `Go toolchain` entry in `components.lock` mirrors it and `tools/lockgate` fails on any mismatch. Toolchain bumps are adoption-rail events (dated lock edit + go.mod change together).
- **stdlib-first.** Any third-party Go module is an S16 adoption: S16.4 checklist plus a `components.lock` entry (with the module path in the entry's `modules` list) land in the same commit as the first import. CI enforces this (§4).
- **Formatting/linting:** `gofmt`-clean and `go vet`-clean, both enforced in CI. No additional linters at B0 (a linter is a dependency; adopt it through the rail or not at all).
- **Package naming:** short, lowercase, no underscores. The S01.1 module seams map to fixed package names, created by their owning packets (B0-2+): `internal/storage`, `internal/scheduler`, `internal/gates`, `internal/ledger`, `internal/eventlog`, `internal/adapters`. Names are fixed here so packets converge; do not invent variants.
- **Doc comments:** standard Go style on exported identifiers; cite spec sections (e.g. "Spec S01.5") where they genuinely clarify a design constraint. No research narration, no changelog prose in code.
- **⚙ settings discipline (S01.10):** no ⚙ value is ever a hardcoded constant. Consumers read the settings registry (built in P3-B0-2) by dotted key. Until the registry exists, leave a named seam: `// TODO(settings): ⚙ <key> per <section>` — never an inline number.
- **Errors:** wrap with `%w`; introduce sentinel/typed errors only where a caller branches on them.
- Open (deferred to owning packets): logging/structured-log shape → B0-3 (journald posture, S01.11); SQLite driver + storage conventions → landed at B0-2, §6; systemd unit generation → B0-3 (generated, not installed — host changes wait for the B0 gate); frontend conventions → B6 (FC-v1).

## 3. Test conventions

- stdlib `testing` only — assertion/mocking libraries are dependencies and none are adopted.
- Tests are colocated `_test.go` files; table-driven where natural; test binaries and fixtures never write outside `t.TempDir()`.
- `go build ./...` and `go test ./...` green is a precondition for every commit — packets never commit red.
- The real `components.lock` and workflow files are validated inside the test suite (`internal/lockfile`) as well as by the standalone gate, so plain `go test ./...` trips on manifest breakage.
- Bring-up measurements (S19.6) are not Go tests: they are coordinator/operator-run probes recording to `P3/measurements/`.

## 4. Adoption rail mechanics (S16, S01.11)

- **Serialization (resolves S16.2 TBD-P3(lock file serialization), P3 choice dated 2026-07-19):** `components.lock` is strict, pretty-printed JSON — one field per line, so review diffs are clean (S16.2's only constraint), and the Go stdlib parses it, so the gate itself requires zero adoptions. Unknown fields are rejected (`internal/lockfile`); a format migration, if ever wanted, is an ordinary dated lock edit.
- **Fields:** the nine S16.2 normative fields, with `license` structured as `{spdx, scope, checked}` (path scope + check date, P-T16-2) and `role` as `{summary, section}`. Documented extension fields, additive only: `modules` (Go module paths an entry covers — the mechanical hook for the bundled-dependency rule) and `notes`. An npm-side equivalent of `modules` is defined at B6, not before.
- **Entry materialization:** the S16.3 table is the binding v0 commitment; a row's lock entry materializes when its component is first consumed (running unit, bundled dependency, toolchain, CI mechanism). TBD-P3 pins resolve per their row text (Go: "pin at scaffold" → resolved at P3-B0-1) or at the first quarterly pass (S16.7, S19.5). Anything outside S16.3 enters only through the S16.4 onboarding checklist; checklist #10 (operator approval) means packet sessions propose and phase gates approve.
- **CI actions are on the rail.** GitHub Actions workflow actions are constituents of the ratified S01.11 CI mechanism, recorded as `kind: toolchain` lock entries and pinned to full 40-hex commit SHAs (the human tag goes in `notes`), so "no new dependency without a lock entry" holds everywhere. Runner images are pinned to exact stable labels (`ubuntu-24.04`); `-latest` and preview labels are floating and never used.
- **Pin discipline:** exact versions only — never `latest`, `*`, or ranges (`^ ~ > < =` are rejected by validation). Where vendored content exists, the pin carries a content hash and upstream LICENSE/NOTICE files are kept in-tree (S16.2); nothing is vendored yet.
- **The gate:** `go run ./tools/lockgate` — locally before commit and as the CI `lock-gate` step. It validates the manifest fields (S16.2), asserts every `go.mod` require is covered by an entry's `modules`, asserts the `go` directive matches the Go-toolchain pin, and asserts every workflow `uses:` is SHA-pinned and entry-covered. The workflow scanner is a line-level tripwire for this repo's own files, not a general YAML parser — keep workflow `uses:` lines simple. The running-unit half of the S16.2 CI rule activates when unit generation lands (B0-3+).
- **Removal discipline:** an entry leaves the lock only by executing its funeral plan, as a dated lock edit with the reason — never silent deletion (S16.2).

## 5. Commit & process conventions (P3)

- **Subject:** `P3-<phase>-<n>: <summary> (<spec sections>)` — e.g. `P3-B0-1: repo scaffold + adoption rail + CONVENTIONS (S01, S16, S19.5)`. Body optional; trailer on every packet commit: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Stage files explicitly — never `git add -A`. Packet sessions never push (the coordinator pushes after validation), never force-push, never edit `Docs/`, `Spec/`, `Research/`, or `P3/STATE.md`, and never read or stage `*-api-key.txt`.
- **CI:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` runs gofmt, vet, build, test, lock-gate on push to `main` and on PRs. The tag-triggered release pipeline (artifact + `SHA256SUMS` + signed tag, S01.11) is a later packet.

## 6. Storage, event-log & settings conventions (P3-B0-2; S02.1–S02.2, S01.10, S18)

- **Driver:** `modernc.org/sqlite` (pin + funeral plan in `components.lock`; S16.3 row resolved at B0-2). `internal/storage` is its only importer — the storage seam (S01.3). **Pin coupling:** `modernc.org/libc` stays exactly at the version in the driver's own `go.mod` (upstream issue #177); driver and libc bump together, never independently.
- **Connection discipline (S02.1):** WAL journal mode, ⚙ `state.synchronous`, ⚙ `state.busy_timeout`, and `foreign_keys=ON` ride the DSN (`_pragma=…`) so every pooled connection is identical; `_txlock=immediate` makes every `sql.Tx` a `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`. The pool is one connection (in-process serialization; the write rate needs no more). `PRAGMA integrity_check` runs at every open. Pragma ⚙ values are read from the settings registry at open with declared defaults; the shell calls `ReapplySettings` once overrides load (S01.6 step 1). Nothing file-copies the live DB; `-wal`/`-shm` sidecars are never deleted or renamed; WAL stays same-host (S02.1).
- **Migrations (S02.1):** numbered one-transaction SQL files `internal/storage/migrations/NNNN_name.sql`, contiguous from `0001`; each applies atomically with its `PRAGMA user_version` bump (`user_version` is the sole version authority). **A migration file, once committed, is immutable** — schema change = new numbered file, never an edit; history is never rewritten.
- **Append-only enforcement:** `run_events` and `settings_events` carry `BEFORE UPDATE`/`BEFORE DELETE` `RAISE(ABORT)` triggers; a future compaction (11.1) enters via its own migration, never ad-hoc writes.
- **Event conventions (S02.2, S02.5):** ordering only by `event_seq` — timestamps are recorded, never ordering (P-T07-4). Platform-scope events have `run_id NULL` (and no generation); run-scoped appends carry the run's generation and are fenced against `runs.generation` (stale AND ahead both rejected). Payloads are validated before persist (valid JSON, ⚙ `state.event_payload_cap`; refs-not-blobs, P-T07-5). Every row carries `user_id` (15.6). State transitions append via `AppendTx` inside the same `WriteTx` as their row update (S02.3).
- **Settings conventions (S01.10, S18):** all declarations live in `internal/settings/index.go` — declare-once; tests pin the index to the S18.5 tallies (118 keys, 33 domains, per-owner counts). Canonical units: unnamed durations → seconds, sizes → bytes; keys whose name embeds a unit (`_s`, `_days`, `_hours`, `_mb`, `_months`, `_usd`) stay integers/floats in that named unit (S18.4 R10: cosmetic under typed declarations). Map-valued families (`local.alias.<duty>`, `watchdog.silence_budget.<run_type>`) are one `TypeMap` key each, subkeys as map keys. Consumers read ⚙ values by dotted key through narrow per-package interfaces (`storage.Settings`, `eventlog.Settings`) satisfied by `*settings.Registry`; composition order: `settings.New()` → `storage.Open` (defaults) → `Migrate` → `eventlog.New` → `Registry.Attach` → `ReapplySettings`. Every write is one transaction: override row + `settings_events` audit row + `settings.changed` event (G3 Def.2); actors are `operator` or `automation` (automation: auto-flagged keys only, value moves only, within bounds — G1 rider 1); numeric bounds are operator-owned, seeded from the ratified clamps; reset-to-default deletes the row.

## 7. Shell, API & unit-generation conventions (P3-B0-3; S01, S15.2–S15.3, S02.1)

- **Composition root:** `internal/shell` realizes §6's order, then the S01.6 ladder: bootstrap config → registry attach + `ReapplySettings` → listener-binding lint (P-T13-2, fail-closed `shell.ErrNonLoopback`; **literal loopback IPs only — hostnames incl. `localhost` rejected**, re-asserted on the bound listener) → bind + serve (front chain tolerates not-ready) → recovery-ladder seam (`shell.RecoveryLadder`, stub until B0-4; the S01.7 wake path reuses the same level-triggered `Reconcile`) → `platform.started` → `sd_notify(READY)` + heartbeat → admission-seam resume. SIGTERM/SIGINT shutdown: STOPPING → stop admission → `platform.stopping` → SSE final drain → graceful HTTP stop → `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` → close (in-process budget 20 s; unit `TimeoutStopSec=90` exceeds it, S01.6). WAL-truncate scheduling is a shell goroutine on ⚙ `state.wal_truncate_interval` (live-apply, re-read per cycle). Maintenance mode is `shell.Maintenance` (`running/draining/maintenance`, ⚙ `shell.drain_grace`, park-at-expiry via the admission seam); no HTTP mutation surface until auth lands (B0-5).
- **Bootstrap config (S01.10):** `<config-dir>/bootstrap.conf`, strict `key = value` lines (`http_addr`, `db_path`; `#` comments; unknown keys fail closed); config dir = `--config-dir` → `$CONFIGURATION_DIRECTORY` → `/etc/sinet`. **Dev posture is the absence of systemd env** (`NOTIFY_SOCKET`, `STATE_DIRECTORY`, `CONFIGURATION_DIRECTORY`), never a build flag; dev flags `--state-dir`/`--http-addr` override per invocation. sd_notify is implemented in `internal/sdnotify` on the stdlib (unixgram write) — never adopt a library for it.
- **Logging (S01.11):** ops logs are stdlib `log/slog`, text handler, **stderr** (journald captures under systemd; stdout stays for tool output). The event log remains the only audit truth. No log-level ⚙ exists — none is ratified; don't invent one.
- **Five seams → packages (S01.3):** storage → `internal/storage` (B0-2); process → one unit per organ — generation in `internal/units`, mode table in `internal/cli`; API → `internal/api`; adapter → `internal/adapters`; adoption → `components.lock` + `internal/lockfile` + `tools/lockgate` (B0-1). The §2 module-seam packages `internal/{scheduler,gates,ledger,adapters}` exist from B0-3 as stubs citing their owning sections (S10/S02/S05/S03); owning packets replace stub internals, package names stay. The shell's lifecycle calls cross seams only through `shell.Admission`/`shell.RecoveryLadder`.
- **API & SSE (S15.2–S15.3, S02.1):** unversioned at v0 (no `/v1`). `/api/health` is the readiness surface: 200 once the S01.6 sequence completes, 503 while starting/stopping, JSON `{ready, mode, version, event_head}` (`event_head` = snapshot-then-tail cursor bootstrap). The one SSE endpoint `/events`: frames `id:` = `event_seq` (sole ordering authority), `event:` = stored type, `data:` = row JSON with payload embedded; resume via `Last-Event-ID`, then `?after_seq`; cursorless connect tails from head. Tailing is short-batch (256/read — not ⚙; S18 has no such key) on a 250 ms poll baseline plus in-process nudge; keepalive comment every ⚙ `obs.sse_keepalive`. Every route runs behind the identity-middleware seam (`api.Authenticator`); the dev implementation is a static `dev` identity, resolution-only, until B0-5. Platform lifecycle events `platform.started`/`platform.stopping` (platform-scope, `user_id` `platform`) are minted provisionally pending the S14 event contract (B5).
- **Unit generation (S01.2, S01.11):** `sinet units` renders the owned unit set + the journald cap drop-in (`journald-sinet.conf`, ⚙ `shell.journal_max_use`) to stdout or `--out DIR`, from **registry defaults** (override-aware regeneration arrives with the operator surfaces); ⚙ `shell.watchdog_sec` renders `WatchdogSec=`. Units with spec-stable invocations (control/broker/portpool: `ExecStart=<bin> <mode>`) are complete; `sinet-engine@`/`sinet-run@` emit as marked **DRAFT** skeletons carrying only spec-fixed directives (run@: `Type=exec` + `RemainAfterExit=yes` + `ExitType=cgroup` + `Restart=no`, S02.5) — their `ExecStart` belongs to S03/S11.8 at B1. Every unit: `User=sinet` (never `DynamicUser=`), the S01.2 hardening set, the A1 hostname constants (`units.Hostname`/`units.FQDN`). Adopted organs' units are never generated (process seam: replace unit + lock entry). **Generation never installs**; `dist/` (git-ignored) is the conventional local output dir; host installs are phase-gate operator decisions. Non-daemon subcommands (`units`, `version`) are tools, not modes: no unit file, never in the reserved-mode table.

## 8. Run FSM, effect journal & recovery conventions (P3-B0-4; S02.3–S02.7, S02.9, S16.5)

- **Package map:** the S02 machinery lands as `internal/run` (S02.3 FSM + S02.6 freshness comparison), `internal/gates` (S02.4 checkpoints + S02.7 effect journal — the S01.1 gates seam its B0-3 stub reserved), `internal/recovery` (S02.5 ladder, implements `shell.RecoveryLadder`). The shell owns *when* the ladder runs: startup step 3 plus a sweep goroutine on ⚙ `recovery.sweep_interval` (live-apply, mirrors the WAL loop).
- **Transition discipline (S02.3):** every `runs.state` change goes through `run.Store` in ONE WriteTx — CAS-guarded UPDATE + `eventlog.AppendTx` — and run creation appends its birth event in the same tx (the run's existence is in the D7 event record from its first instant). The resume edge parked→running bumps `generation` and appends at the new one; fork is the takeover bump: crash event at the old generation → old-row generation bump (the fence) → successor row + `run.forked` event at generation+1, all one tx. The ratified edge set lives in `run.transitions` with per-edge citations; `crashed` is terminal-but-supersedable (harvest/fork/tombstone/finalize), the other four terminals admit nothing.
- **Fork lineage (migration 0002; S02.5 step 3):** `runs.parent_run_id` (supersession link), `runs.dispatch_id` (durable one-dispatch-per-interruption id, `<parent>#g<newGen>`, partial-unique index = the double-start CAS), `runs.recovery_attempts` (lineage-cumulative, checked against ⚙ `recovery.max_attempts` before each fork). Successor run ids are `<parent>.g<newGen>`; successors enter `queued` (the work was already admitted; queue-row materialization is S10's, B1). Step 3's "with backoff" rides the pass cadence (⚙ `recovery.sweep_interval`) until the scheduler owns re-dispatch timing (B1) — no backoff ⚙ exists and none was invented.
- **Run event types** `run.created` / `run.state` / `run.forked` / `run.wedged` / `run.harvest` / `run.checkpoint` are provisional pending the S14 event contract (B5), extending §7's lifecycle-naming note. Run-scoped event rows are attributed to the run's **owner** (15.6); the acting principal (`platform` for ladder/shell acts, `run.ActorPlatform`) rides the payload.
- **Effect journal (S02.7, gh-aw safe-outputs shape S16.5 — effects gate ≠ quality gate):** payloads are stored canonicalized (UseNumber decode → sorted-key compact re-marshal; platform-internal consistency, not cross-system canonicality) and `payload_hash` = sha256 hex over that form. `executing` commits in its own tx BEFORE the provider call; `idempotency_key` = the effect UUID, set at first BeginExecute, never changed. Approval expiry (⚙ `effects.approval_expiry`) or payload drift at execution **commits** the row back to `proposed` (Terraform saved-plan semantics — the reset must outlive the failed call) and reports `ErrApprovalExpired`/`ErrPayloadDrift`. Reconcile of in-doubt rows: A/B/C → `approved` with key + attempts intact (replay dedups at the provider; class C searches first when attempts > 0), D → `unknown` + decision card (P-T07-3). Journal transitions are effects-row updates, not `run_events` appends; composition with FSM transitions uses the `…Tx` variants.
- **Ladder discipline (S02.5):** level-triggered, passes serialized; suspend-aware grace (⚙ `recovery.wake_grace`) applies on a process's first pass and on a wall-clock jump > 2× ⚙ `recovery.sweep_interval` between passes (the S01.7 clock-jump wake detection; logind D-Bus wiring is a later packet — needs an adoption decision, no stdlib D-Bus). DEAD needs proof (corpse-failed) or a dead/absent lease — a live lease is always honored, and WEDGED is pause-and-flag (once per stall episode), never auto-kill (D1.3). Crash + bound decision (fork / tombstone / finalize) commit in one tx so no crashed run is ever left without its decision; ⚙ `recovery.stale_finalize` applies to crash-interruptions only — parked-on-gate runs are "blocked, not failed" and route through the S02.6 freshness pass instead. Checkpoints are writable only in `running`/`draining`. B0 observation seams `recovery.UnitLiveness`/`recovery.HarvestSource` default to observing nothing; real systemd + engine-store probes arrive with run units and adapters (B1).
- **Kill-9 harness (S02.9):** a standing conformance entry in `internal/recovery` — the test binary re-execs itself (`SINET_CRASH_SCENARIO`) as a child that drives real machinery to a nasty window and dies by self-SIGKILL; the parent asserts `integrity_check` ok, committed state intact, the interrupted tx invisible, one pass classifying every non-terminal run, fencing inert, per-class effect resolution, gap-free `event_seq`, and ask-row survival. The suspend-cycle companion injects clock deltas through the ladder's `Now` seam.

## 9. Auth conventions (P3-B0-5; S01.9, S01.4, S15.2)

- **Package split:** `internal/auth` is the S01.9 data layer (sessions, argon2id PINs, device grants — AuthZ enforced here, per S01.9 "enforced in the control plane's data layer": accessors take the acting user and check the one role bit against the users row); `internal/api` keeps the seam (`api.Authenticator`, production impl `SessionAuthenticator`) plus the login/session endpoints under `/api/auth/*` (S15.2: those endpoints are S01.9's; exact paths are P3's within that bound). Migration `0003` adds the PIN columns, `sessions`, `device_grants`.
- **Route classes:** the pre-session surface is exactly `/api/health`, `GET /api/auth/session`, `GET /api/auth/users`, `POST /api/auth/login`, and `POST /api/auth/users` (bootstrap window only) — reaching it already proves tailnet membership (S01.9 layer 1). Everything else, `/events` included, requires an authenticated identity, fail-closed 401; an internal resolution failure is 500, never an anonymous downgrade.
- **Sessions (layer 3):** server-side rows; the cookie (`sinet_session`, HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax for S15.11 deep links, Secure outside dev posture) carries a 256-bit bearer token stored only as its SHA-256. Expiry is absolute wall-clock (a suspend never extends a login); expired rows delete on sight plus a login-time sweep. **No auth ⚙ keys exist in S18** — session TTL 30 d, 5-attempt ceiling, 15-min lockout, PIN length 4–128, and the RFC 9106 argon2id parameters are code constants (the `sseBatchSize` precedent, §7), flagged to the B0 gate; dialing any of them is an S00.9 amendment + S18 sweep. Credentials are PHC strings, so parameters ride the stored hash and can move without invalidating PINs.
- **Device hint (layer 2):** only `Tailscale-User-Login` is parsed (the one injected header S01.9 assigns meaning); it prefills (`/api/auth/session` hint block) and completes login solely through an operator grant row (`device_grants`, one per device account — the header IS the whole device signal). Grant-login never touches the PIN failure counter; the counter is shared between login and step-up (same secret, same guessing surface). No-grant default = shared device, PIN always required (G3 Def.1).
- **Step-up (High-tier re-prompt):** no stored elevation state — S01.9's "never inherited from an idle session" is implemented as verify-at-act: credential/grant/user-admin verbs re-prompt the actor's own PIN in the same request (`VerifyPIN`, purpose-tagged `auth.reprompt` events), and the B2+ approvals family routes High-tier answers through the same call.
- **Audit:** every auth act appends in the same tx as its row change — provisional types `auth.{login,login_failed,logout,pin_set,grant,grant_revoked,reprompt,reprompt_failed,user_created}` (pending S14, extending §7's naming note), owner-attributed per 15.6; acts without an owning row (unknown-user attempts) attribute to `platform` with the attempted id in the payload. Failure responses stay collapsed (401) — the event log carries the precise reason.
- **Bootstrap & reserved ids:** while `users` is empty, one anonymous create is allowed and MUST be an operator (D10 needs a holder from the first user); `platform` and `dev` are reserved ids no person row may take. The dev fallback identity (dev posture only, absent systemd env, §7 — detection now `shell.devPosture()`) is resolution-only: it reads, but every admin verb refuses it.
- **Rail:** `golang.org/x/crypto` v0.54.0 adopted at B0-5 (S01.9 names argon2id; stdlib has none) — lock entry with S16.4 walk, golden-vector conformance test in `internal/auth`, companion `golang.org/x/sys` bump v0.46→v0.47 (coverage stays with the driver entry); operator ratification (#10) queued for the B0 gate.

## 10. Adapter & engine conventions (P3-B1-1; S03, S02.4, S16.3)

- **Package map:** `internal/adapters` owns the D3 contract (S03.1 verbs: start/stream/checkpoint/pause/resume/cancel/report_usage) **and** the platform-side `Driver` that persists what the contract yields (run_events, S02.4 checkpoint rows, `engine_sessions` + transcript copy-aside, ask rows). One package per substrate beneath it: `internal/adapters/claudecli` (Anthropic lane); the opencode adapter lands with its consuming packet under `internal/adapters/` too. Substrate names on `runs.substrate` are the package constants (`claude-cli`, `opencode`); lane names `anthropic`/`zai`/`local` (S03.2).
- **Engine events in run_events:** adapter-observed events append as `engine.<kind>` over the closed S03.1 kind set (message/usage/rate_limit/gate_ask/tool_result/done) — names provisional pending the S14 event contract (B5), extending §7/§8's note. Unknown ENGINE frame types are logged and skipped, never fatal and never minted as new platform kinds (S03.1 MUST). Payload excerpts bound at `adapters.ExcerptCap` (4 KiB, plain constant — the §7 `sseBatchSize` precedent); full content lives in the engine transcript + copy-aside (P-T07-5).
- **Paid-call bookkeeping:** each per-call usage event commits `engine.usage` + the S02.4 checkpoint row + its `run.checkpoint` event in ONE WriteTx; run-total usage rows (terminal envelope) ride `engine.done` + the Outcome, never a checkpoint (D7 is per paid call). Ledger-revision block stays an empty seam until S05 (B2); artifact-snapshot block fills via `Driver.Snapshot` when workspace machinery lands.
- **Conformance tiers (S03.3) & THE sanctioned skip class:** every engine adapter ships a conformance suite in three marked tiers — **F** replay fixtures (always run; CI has no engine), **R** real-engine zero-cost (auto-runs when the engine binary is installed), **L** real-engine paid (only under `SINET_LIVE_SMOKE=1`, one minimal call). Tier R/L absence-skips print `SANCTIONED SKIP (CONVENTIONS §10)` — this is the one skip class allowed in a green battery. Suites assert behavior, never docs/help text (S03.3 rule 4; live-proven: 2.1.215 dropped `--max-turns` from `--help` while still parse-accepting it).
- **Pin discipline:** each adapter exports its `Pin`, test-coupled to the `components.lock` engine entry; a pin↔installed delta is reported LOUDLY (test notice + lock-entry note + packet report) and never silently retargeted — reconciling is the operator's S03.3 deliberate-bump procedure. Fixtures record engine behavior with provenance (spike record or live re-record, dated).
- **Credential hygiene (D2):** owner credential refs are PATHS handed through env (`CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` = the per-user config root on the Claude lane), never read, logged, or embedded; lowering scrubs `ANTHROPIC_*`, `CLAUDECODE`, `CLAUDE_CODE_*`, `AI_AGENT`, `CLAUDE_EFFORT`, `CLAUDE_PID`, and ambient `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` from the child env (S03.5 env channel + subscription-auth protection). Broker-mediated injection replaces the dev-mode ref at B1-3 (S11).
- **Gate hook:** `sinet engine-hook --ctl <dir>` is a tool subcommand (§7 sense) compiled into lowered settings for gated tools (S03.4); it exchanges state through the ctl dir only (config.json / fires.log / answers/). Serialize-by-deny turn-window reset is adapter-maintained and heuristic until the B1-4 spike battery reconfirms the mechanism live (TBD-BRINGUP).
- **Dev-mode confinement seam:** B1-1 spawns run unconfined (own process group, scrubbed env, isolated cwd + platform-owned WorkDir) — the sandbox/broker wrap arrives at B1-3 (S11) around the same `StartRequest`; nothing else may widen the dev-mode posture.

## 11. Metering & scheduler conventions (P3-B1-2; S10, S02.2)

- **Package map:** `internal/metering` is the **S10 consumption ledger** — a READ MODEL over the `checkpoints` table (S10.1 "the checkpoint's usage block IS the ledger row"; aggregations are queries, not new state), plus the `receipts` materializer, the consumption-pressure gauge, the price-table seam, and the Anthropic utilization overlay. It is **NOT** `internal/ledger` (that seam is the S05 *Task Context Ledger*, B2). `internal/scheduler` owns the S10.7 claim loop, the five-class limit taxonomy (S10.5, `limits.go`), and the workload-class priority ladder (S10.7, `workload.go`). The scheduler is `shell.Admission` (replacing the B0 stub); it never imports `adapters` — the `Dispatcher` seam keeps it engine-agnostic (S01.3).
- **Price table ships EMPTY at v0.** The `PriceTable` seam (`EffectiveDatedTable`) carries the S10.3 semantics (effective-dated rows, the never-price-a-flat-lane-from-$0 guard), but the shipped genai-prices `data.json` seed is a vendored-content S16.3 adoption (TBD-P3 pin; operator #10) and is a **flagged follow-up**, not this stdlib-only packet. An empty table prices every row **UNPRICED (tier-5)** — exactly S10.1's honest posture ("no row silently prices to $0"); receipts still show full measured token breakdowns. `directUse` cites `Spec/benchmark-preregistration-v1.md §13` and never restates its numbers.
- **Derived classifications at B1-2:** `purpose_tag` = `execution` for every row (a run is one invocation; ceremony/verification are pipeline stages S06/S07, B2). `approximation_tier` = 1 (measured) when the checkpoint carries token counts, else 5 (unknown). Both derive in one place so B2 changes the input, not the shape.
- **Two currencies / metered-exception list:** `MeteredExceptions` is **EMPTY at v0** (G1 P7) → every lane flat-rate → api-equivalent currency; the currency visibly flips only for an explicitly metered lane (P-T01-3). Dollar-based routing between flat lanes is a D5 violation and is never coded — claim order uses class + aging + pressure, never dollars.
- **Non-⚙ structural/policy constants** (S18 ratifies no key — the §7 `sseBatchSize`/§9 auth-constant precedent, flagged to the B1 gate): lane concurrency `defaultLaneConcurrency = 1` when no `lanes` row (observed concurrency starts at 1; the row is DATA seeded by `SetLaneCap`); the workload-class aging biases (`classBias`/`agingGap`, workload.go) implementing the S10.7 ladder ("aging so background never starves"); the claim-loop `defaultPoll`. Making any of these operator-tunable is an S00.9 amendment adding S18 rows.
- **⚙ consumed here** (by dotted key, never hardcoded): `pressure.cache_read_weight`, `pressure.bg_admit_stop`, `budget.background_window_fraction`, `meter.value_divergence_alarm`, `limit.retry_cap`, `limit.retry_budget_ratio`, `limit.probe_interval_max`, and cross-cutting `recovery.dead_after` (the claim lease deadline, owned S02). **Seams referenced but not consumed:** `scheduler.missed_slot_default` (dormant, v1 schedule surface, S10.7/S19), `arbitration.background_cpuweight` (S10.9 local arbitration; slice mechanism S01, VRAM broker S12, B4), `freshness.hold_vs_park` (cache-window-aware resume timing).
- **Scheduler ↔ engine seam:** every run reaches an engine only via `Enqueue` (sole ingress, owner-attributed) → CAS claim under per-(user,lane) slots → `Dispatcher.Dispatch` — fire-and-forget is banned (S16.6). The control plane's `runDispatcher` registers **no adapters** at B1-2 (confined execution is B1-3, S11; ingress is intake S06, B2), so the dev-mode queue stays empty and no engine is spawned from the control plane. Receipts materialize on `run.IsTerminal` (idempotent, one per run) atomically with the queue-row settle.
- **Utilization overlay is trust-gated.** `ParseUnifiedHeaders` reads the `anthropic-ratelimit-unified-*` family and `TrustParkTiming()` is **always false at B1-2** (TBD-BRINGUP: value scale + `7d_oi` semantics need the S19.6 observation). Class-2 park resume times come from the STREAM signal (`rate_limit_event.resetsAt`), never the headers, until the window closes. The header SOURCE is the credential-injection proxy (B1-3, S11); the overlay records as a platform-scope `meter.utilization` event (provisional naming pending S14).
- **Limit classifier is a pure, fixture-tested component** (P-T08-2): `Classify(sig, cfg)` is total and side-effect-free; **Class 4 (auth/policy) is checked FIRST** so an auth event can never fall through to a retry-park (the named worst case). Per-lane fixtures live in `limits_test.go`.

## 12. Sandbox & credential-broker conventions (P3-B1-3; S11, S01.2/S01.6, S16.3)

- **Package map:** `internal/sandbox` composes the S11.1 per-run stack (the S01.3 process seam, sandbox side): `srt.go` (**the ADOPTED, ratified primary composition path** — Anthropic's `sandbox-runtime`: `SrtConfigFor` generates srt's single-JSON config from the class, `SrtInvocation` builds `srt --settings <cfg> -- <engine>`, `BuildPlan` selects srt-when-present / native-when-absent), `sandbox.go` (the **native funeral-plan fallback**: class profiles C0–C2 + `Compose` → bwrap argv), `probe.go` (`Probe`/`Capabilities`, S16.3 probe-at-compose + srt discovery), `seccomp.go` (pure-Go BPF, x/sys constants — the fallback's seccomp), `landlock.go` (ruleset-as-data + enforcement seam), `admit.go` (Rule-of-Two + P-T05-1 helper-tightening), `confiner.go` (`Composer` implementing `adapters.Confiner`, reads the 4 ⚙ keys), `run_launch.go` (the S11.8 `sinet run-launch` host entry). `internal/broker` is the S11.5 credential broker (S01.2 owns the unit): `store.go` (per-person encrypted store), `broker.go` (UDS server + SO_PEERCRED + decision/delivery split), `client.go` (`Client` + `EnvInjector`), `mode.go` (`sinet broker`). **`sandbox → adapters` is the only import direction** (adapters owns the `Confiner`/`SpawnSpec` seam types); no adapter imports `sandbox`.
- **The composed stack (S11.1), outward-to-inward:** `sinet-run@` unit [host, S11.8] → **bubblewrap** (user/mount/PID/UTS/IPC + empty netns; workspace + ro caches) → **seccomp-BPF** (loaded via `bwrap --seccomp` fd) → **Landlock** (ABI 8) → engine. The **boundary is bwrap namespaces + the empty netns** (S11.4 primary default-drop); **seccomp and Landlock are defense-in-depth**. bwrap MUST be the system `/usr/bin/bwrap` (`SystemBwrap`) — the shipped AppArmor profile attaches to that path (S11.2 rider); never a vendored/renamed copy.
- **Probe-at-compose (S16.3 OS-mechanism discipline):** `Compose` fails closed (`ErrNotComposable`) when the MANDATORY boundary (bwrap + functional unprivileged userns) is absent — named errors, never a silent unconfined run. Defense-in-depth absence (no seccomp/Landlock) is a **sanctioned skip**, not a failure: tests print `SANCTIONED SKIP (S16.3)` when `Caps().Available()` is false (the one skip class allowed in a green battery, extending §10). The host probe re-runs at control-plane startup (logged) and an OS upgrade re-runs it before admission.
- **Empty-env, deny-by-default (S11.1), not scrub:** the sandbox starts from `--clearenv` and re-adds only the lowered engine vars via `--setenv`; nothing is bound unless listed, so credential stores are unreachable by construction. Platform config is bound **read-only** (`--ro-bind`, the P-T09-1 deny-writes-to-config mitigation, symlink-resolved); the S03.4 gate control dir is the one **read-write** exchange bind. Per-class profiles, the seccomp profile, and the Landlock ruleset are **STRUCTURAL** (versioned in code), **not ⚙** (S11 settings note).
- **⚙ consumed (by dotted key, never hardcoded):** `sandbox.egress_deny_cidrs`, `sandbox.block_outbound_doh`, `sandbox.c2_registry_allowlist`, `sandbox.model_egress_tls_terminate` — read by `Composer.EgressPolicyFor` for egress-needing classes and compiled into the confinement DATA. C1 (empty netns) reads none.
- **The confinement seam (extends §10):** `adapters.StartRequest.Confiner` (nil ⇒ unconfined B1-1 dev spawn; set ⇒ the adapter builds its `*exec.Cmd` through `Confine`) and `StartRequest.CredInject` (broker injection at spawn) are both `json:"-"` — confinement is **recompiled every run** from control-plane data, and the resolved secret is **never** on the struct, in a park record, in logs, or in the DB (S11.6/S11.7; D2/S11.5). The confined dev path and the `sinet run-launch` host path share one composition core (`Compose`).
- **Broker (S11.5) — ssh-agent posture, never git-credential-helper:** per-user UDS; **SO_PEERCRED** attests the asker (a foreign uid is refused before any op). Two credential kinds enforce the two postures: **`engine-cred`** is DELIVERED at spawn (`Resolve` → env injection, S01.6 "engines receive credentials at start"); **`signing-key`** is used result-only (`Sign` → HMAC; the key NEVER leaves — outward effects route to the 4.2 gated flow). The **decision path holds zero secrets** (the dispatch switch + kind/destination checks); only delivery touches plaintext. Credentials are **audience-bound** (AES-GCM AAD = user|profile|kind); the store dir is `0700`, `master.key` `0600`, host-only.
- **Adoptions (CORRECTED at P3-B1-3R — supersedes the B1-3 mislabel above):** **srt** (`sandbox-runtime`) is **ADOPTED as the ratified PRIMARY composition path** — S11.1 "Adoption" **binds** srt for the bwrap+seccomp+proxy core (ratified G2 D2.2); direct composition is only the S16.3 **funeral plan**. B1-3 wrongly shipped the funeral plan as if it were the design and materialized no srt entry — a spec-conformance defect (adopt-don't-fork + gate-ratification), fixed here (operator Path A, B1 gate 2026-07-20). The **`sandbox-runtime` `library` lock entry is materialized**, pin **LIVE-verified at build time** (v0.0.66; Apache-2.0 path-scoped at the pinned ref), **no `modules` claim** (a Node CLI, not a Go module — like the claude engine + os-mechanism entries); #10 operator ratification already granted. The native direct composition (`Compose` → bwrap argv) is **RETAINED and relabeled to its correct role: the S16.3 funeral-plan / fallback**, never a substitute for adoption. The **`os-mechanism`** row remains (both paths ride bwrap+seccomp+Landlock+netns). **sops/age** are still NOT consumed at B1 (dev-mode at-rest store = stdlib AES-256-GCM, zero new deps; systemd-creds + sops/age lands at a host-install gate; coheres with operator age-escrow item 6). **Zero new Go dependencies** — srt is an external CLI, not a Go module.
- **srt-when-present, native-fallback-when-absent (B1-3R):** srt **activation is host-dependent** — srt must be installed on the host, a change that **batches at the B2 gate** alongside the egress substrate + run@ install (exactly like B1-3 deferred those). `Probe` discovers srt (PATH, or the `SINET_SRT_PATH` override — the operator / B2-activation knob **and** the hermetic test-injection seam); `BuildPlan` composes **via srt when present, via the native funeral-plan fallback when absent**, and the confiner/launcher **log which path is active**. So dev-mode confinement keeps working now (the native fallback runs at B1 — no regression) and the native code is the spec-sanctioned fallback, not a rogue primary. The MANDATORY boundary (bwrap + functional userns) gates **both** paths (srt itself rides bwrap). The **live srt-wrapping smoke defers to the B2 host batch** (a deferred seam, like B1-3's egress substrate); at B1 the srt path is exercised with a **stub `srt`** under `t.TempDir()` asserting the generated config JSON + invocation.
- **srt config generation (B1-3R):** `SrtConfigFor(class, spawn, egress)` is a **pure** function emitting srt's real schema at the pinned version (`network:{allowedDomains,deniedDomains,strictAllowlist}` + `filesystem:{denyRead,allowRead,allowWrite,denyWrite}`) — a generated config for an **unmodified** adopted tool, not a Sinet schema. Mapping: no-egress classes ⇒ `deniedDomains:["*"]` + strict (deny-all); egress classes ⇒ `allowedDomains` from the ⚙-compiled `EgressPolicy` + strict. srt does **hostname allowlisting** (the S11.4 convenience layer); the **IP:port default-drop that CONTAINS** (⚙ `egress_deny_cidrs` + `block_outbound_doh`) stays the **host nftables layer** (deferred with the egress substrate), not expressible as srt domain patterns. Filesystem: workspace read-only (C1) / read-write (C2 overlay upper), config paths **deny-write** (P-T09-1, srt/bwrap resolves symlinks), `/tmp` ephemeral-writable, credential stores **deny-read** (D2). srt's `credentials` **injection block** (TLS-terminating model-egress substitution) is **omitted at B1**, deferred with the credential-injection proxy to B2 (⚙ `sandbox.model_egress_tls_terminate`). srt forwards its env to the wrapped engine, so the **lowered engine env IS the srt wrapper env** (vs the native path's `--clearenv`/`--setenv` inside bwrap) — the sentinel-not-real-token rule (D2/S11.5) holds on both.
- **Seams / deferred (all host changes or larger components):** the host **egress substrate** (nftables default-DROP + host proxy, `sinet-egress-setup`, S11.4/S11.8) — egress-needing classes (C0/C2) carry their allow-list as DATA and compose with an empty netns until it lands; the **credential-injection proxy** (TLS-terminating model-egress substitution, `sandbox.model_egress_tls_terminate`, S11.5) — for a confined engine the sandbox holds a **sentinel** and the proxy substitutes the real token (a B1-4/later component; it is also the SOURCE for §11's utilization overlay); **Landlock live enforcement** (x/sys v0.47 has no Landlock wrappers — raw-syscall/library adoption later); the **run@ template install + name-scoped polkit grant** (S11.8 Shape B — operator gate step, no host changes at B1); the **srt host install + live srt-wrapping smoke** (B2 host batch — the srt-config generation + probe-selection + stub-verified invocation are LIVE now; only the on-host `srt` binary and its live wrap defer, so the native funeral-plan fallback runs at B1, B1-3R).
- **Clearly-implied readings (section-cited):** (1) **seccomp-parity disposition (B1-3R)** — srt (adopted primary) supplies its own Node-safe **positive** syscall allow-list (its apply-seccomp binary); `seccomp.go` is the **native fallback's** profile, a default-ALLOW filter that KILLS the S11.1-named dangerous syscalls (ptrace/process_vm_readv/writev). S11 does **not** demand the fallback be brought to positive-allowlist parity — S11.1's "allowlist-only" STRUCTURAL rule is scoped to "mounts, tools, egress", and S11.1 describes the syscall layer by the calls it "kills"; the `allowProgram` seam remains if parity is ever wanted (not gold-plated). (2) The P-T05-1 tightening check is **per-axis domination** (ladder rank, egress reach, credential access, workspace-write) rather than a single ladder comparison, because the classes are not totally ordered (C0 carries a scoped credential + single-host egress that C1 lacks). (3) S11.8's `/usr/lib/sinet/run-launch` is realized as **the multi-call binary in the `run-launch` mode** (S01.5 one-binary rule; the same reconciliation S11.8 applied to S01.2's run-unit wording). (4) srt's config is generated **faithfully but minimally** at B1 (network + filesystem per the S11.6 class table) — broader deny-then-allow tightening of srt's permissive-read default, and the credential-injection block, are tuned/added against the **deferred B2 live smoke**, never guessed blind (the engine's real read set is only observable live), so B2 hardens and never loosens. (5) The **C0 single-host** egress list is not carried on the B1 confiner seam (it arrives with the worker record when that seam widens) — inert anyway until the deferred host egress substrate lands; the pure `SrtConfigFor` unit test covers the C0 mapping. (6) The srt invocation uses `--` to terminate srt's option parsing before the engine argv (the robust shape; the exact need for `--` is a B2 live-smoke detail).

## 13. Task Context Ledger & injection conventions (P3-B2-1; S05, S02.2, S02.4)

- **Package map:** `internal/ledger` is the **S05 Task Context Ledger** (the S01.1 module seam its B0-3 stub reserved) — the per-task document + write rules (`ledger.go`), the event-sourced Store with the session-verb surface and control-plane ops (`store.go`), and fresh-context-per-stage assembly + trace manifest + injection materialization (`assemble.go`). It is NOT the S10 consumption ledger (`internal/metering`, §11). Imports: `storage` + `eventlog` only; the adapter Driver reaches it through a func seam (`Driver.LedgerRevision` ← `(*ledger.Store).CheckpointRef`), no package import either direction.
- **Persistence is the event stream (S02.2):** no ledger table exists — every accepted write appends ONE `ledger_update` run-event (type name fixed by S02.2) whose payload carries `{change, content_hash, ledger}` with the **full document**; current state = the latest event for the task (joined across the task's runs, so fork successors continue one ledger), point reads by exact `ledger_version` (`AtVersion`). ⚙ `state.event_payload_cap` is the structural small-by-design bound: an oversize write is rejected whole at the validate-before-persist gate, never truncated. Writes are one WriteTx (read run → read current doc → mutate → version+1 → `AppendTx`), row attributed to the run's owner, acting principal in `change.actor` (§8 pattern).
- **Fencing split:** session verbs (`SessionVerbs(runID, stage, generation)`) append at the session's **asserted** (spawn-time) generation so the S02.5 fence rejects superseded stage sessions; control-plane ops (`SetObjective`/`SetConstraints`/`RecordDecision`/`SetVerified`/`SetPlanVersion`) write at the run's current generation (single-writer acts, not resumable zombies).
- **Write rules at the write layer (S05.1):** pinned §1/§2 change only under a NEW spec version (`ErrPinnedImmutable`; §2 additionally records + requires the header's current spec version); a session-claimed `verified` is structurally rejected (`ErrVerifiedFromSession`), a verified item is closed to session writes, and `SetVerified` demands `done_unverified` + a verdict evidence ref (S07 seam). Decisions/artifacts are append-only (no edit/delete API exists). Stage-close gate = `CheckStageClose`: every assigned item `done_unverified`/`verified`/`blocked` or **handed forward = listed in `state.next_actions`** (the only ledger structure naming successor work).
- **Assembly (S05.4):** `Assemble` is pure lookup over platform-owned facts (`SliceQuery{TaskID, Owner, Stage, Clean}` — no agent-supplied identifier ever keys a source); blocks collect deterministically (sources Knowledge→Conventions→Worker→Plan, ledger projection, Extra) then stability-sort by precedence bucket `house→project→user→task→stage`; 8.9 precedence rides **explicit labels** on every block/manifest entry, never ordering. Pinned sections render **always and whole** through the one shared renderer (brief + placed file + re-injection byte-identical); non-pinned ledger sections only when non-empty. One `context.manifest` event per assembly (name provisional pending S14, extending §7/§8's note) with the S05.4 entry schema `{item_id, source_path, content_hash, version, selector_rule, precedence_label}`; mid-stage re-injections append `kind:"reinjection"` entries (`AppendReinjectionManifest`). Clean mode (verification, S05.4 exception): ledger projection = `objective_ac` only, user-overlay items structurally dropped, learned excluded; what else a verify stage receives is S07's (B2-3), passed via `Extra`. Shim-drift check runs at every assembly (CLAUDE.md vs canonical `@AGENTS.md`), surfacing a finding on the result AND the manifest event — never silent (P-T04-2).
- **Injection channel (Claude lane, B1-4 spike 2026-07-20):** stage-brief body = `BriefText` via prompt assembly (`CompiledWorker.Prompt`/`SystemPromptAppend`, wired at B2-4); pinned survival = `PlacePinned` (WorkDir `pinned-context.md`, read-only under confinement) + `CompiledWorker.SessionStartContextPath` compiling a `SessionStart` hook (matcher = spike-observed source enum `startup|resume|compact`, a per-pin canary fact) that re-emits the file verbatim as `additionalContext` via the spike-recorded `hookSpecificOutput` contract (`sinet engine-hook --ctl <dir> --session-start <path>`, sharing the S03.4 hook plumbing). Fires log to `<ctl>/session_start.fires.log` (the one rw exchange dir, §12); the platform turns fires into re-injection manifest events. PreCompact **blocking** is an available per-pin bonus (spike M2), deliberately unwired: containment stays the version-independent primary (S05.7).
- **⚙ posture:** B2-1 consumes no new ⚙ key (the payload cap rides `eventlog`). The declared S05 keys `context.stage_fit_target` / `context.stage_overflow_threshold` / `context.recitation_interval_turns` belong to stage-runtime budget/recitation machinery (B2-2+/B2-4 consumers); `context.conventions_max_lines` to the S1.6 conventions-generation task — declared since B0-2, deliberately unconsumed here, never hardcoded anywhere.
- **Implied readings (section-cited, beyond the rules above):** ledger birth is implicit at the first accepted write (S05.1 defines no birth verb; the event stream is the authoritative copy, S02.2); control-plane change names `ledger.objective_ac`/`ledger.constraints`/`ledger.plan_version`/`ledger.verify` extend the spec's session-verb vocabulary (the spec names only the four session verbs); the checkpoint block (c) is `{"ledger_version":N,"sha256":…}` — version + content hash, S02.4(c)'s "content or content-hash" with content already self-contained in `run_events`.

## 14. Intake pipeline conventions (P3-B2-2; S06, S02.8, S05 seam)

- **Package map:** `internal/intake` is the S06 pipeline — Stage 0 triage + P47 rule layer (`pipeline.go` Start, `triggers.go`), the interview taxonomies + Clearance (`taxonomy.go`), the SPEC/PLAN artifact pair (`artifact.go`), the Stage-2 deterministic spine (`spine.go`), cards/asks (`cards.go`, `state.go`), the stage driver + Stage-4 approval/claims (`pipeline.go`, `answer.go`), post-approval deltas (`delta.go`), and the S05.4 `Sources.Plan` implementation (`plansource.go`, `intake.PlanSource`). Imports: storage/eventlog/run/ledger only. Model duties are seams per S06.10 — `Planner`/`Critic` (planning model; wired to real engine sessions at B2-4), `Classifier`/`SpotCheck` (local duty aliases, B4), `Utility` (utility model), `Registry` (S13/S1.6, B4) — absent seams degrade as the spec directs (classifier fail-closed → high tier; optional duties skipped, never faked; a missing Critic at standard+ is `ErrSeamMissing`, never a silent skip).
- **Pipeline state is event-sourced** as `intake.state` run-events (full-state payloads, current = latest by `event_seq` — the §13 ledger_update pattern; NO intake table, no migration). Artifact bodies never ride payloads (refs+sha256, P-T07-5). One intake run per task (`<task>.intake`, created with the task row + first state event in one tx); stage work requires `running` (admission is the scheduler's — dev harnesses walk the FSM); gates park the run, answers resume in place (parked→running bumps generation per §8). Delta-decision measurement hooks are `intake.delta_decision` events; both names provisional pending S14 (extending §7/§8/§13's note).
- **Ledger writes (S05 boundary):** Stage 0/1/3 artifacts land via `SessionVerbs` (stages `triage`/`plan`/`critique`); platform findings and human choices via `RecordDecision` (spine findings platform-authored; approvals/drops/dismissals human-authored). On Approve: conditional idempotent pins `SetObjective` (frozen ACs, spec-vN) → `SetConstraints` (+ registry danger zones) → `SetPlanVersion` (`spec-vN/plan-vM`, the freshness fingerprint member) → approval decision with artifact hashes. The ledger never nests inside an intake WriteTx (single-connection pool): decisions recording composes strictly before/after, never inside.
- **Artifacts (S06.6):** markdown+frontmatter of record + JSON sidecar, written atomically under `ArtifactRoot/<task>/`; a load verifies sha256 AND byte-identical re-render (resume-from-artifact integrity). Draft files are immutable once written; approval freezes NEW `*.approved.md` files (crash window between file writes and the closing tx stays consistent); versions: plan bumps every emission, spec only on content change.
- **Seeds (TBD-P3, S19.5):** interview taxonomies ship in code via `SeedTaxonomies()` — software = the ClarifyCodeBench 10-type taxonomy (arXiv:2607.00711 Table 2, live-verified 2026-07-20; weights evidence-informed: failed types 12 > base 10/8 > natively-handled 6), generic fallback drafted at P3-B2-2; P47 rules via `SeedTriggers()` = the S06.3 table verbatim (cue phrases word-boundary, currency patterns; P47-6/-11 classifier-detected). Both are versioned, operator-editable via strict-JSON `LoadTaxonomy`/`LoadTriggers` overrides; formal 8.3-gate entry waits for S09 (B3); **seed content is a B2-gate ratification item**.
- **Claims (S02.8):** written at plan approval in the closing tx — W row from the union of step write-sets (unbounded ⇒ ⚙ `claims.default_write`; declared-set with nothing declared still takes `**`), optional R row; glob-intersection vs other tasks' active W claims in the same project is conservative (literal-prefix overlap; `**` intersects all — over-sequencing is the safe direction), overlap ⇒ status `waiting` + a post-commit decision entry; delta approval supersedes old rows (`superseded`) and writes fresh ones. Queueing behind a holder is the scheduler's.
- **⚙ consumed** (dotted key, never hardcoded): the 7 S06 keys (`intake.zero_interaction_cost_usd` per-user via `FloatFor`, 3 clearance floors, `size_recheck_factor`, `coverage_autofix_rounds`, `critique_revise_rounds`) + `freshness.max_age` (the folded alias of `intake.approval_stale_hours`, S18.4 R2 — pending-approval staleness) + `claims.default_write`. **Non-⚙ constant:** `maxQuestionsPerCard = 4` — S06.5 fixes "up to 4 questions per card" in ratified text, S18 declares no key (§7 precedent; falls under the standing settings-tab directive).
- **Clearly-implied readings (section-cited):** (1) Stage-0 floors observe registry+classifier inputs only — S06.2 pins the five floor classes, not a request-text cue lexicon; Stage 2(b) is the deterministic backstop from the PLAN's declared contents, D7 gating regardless. (2) Clearance counts assumption-conversions as resolved (S06.5's three resolution kinds verbatim); floor-reached leftovers stay unresolved and surface as planner assumptions, band/force-proceed convert all (each origin-labeled). (3) The trivial-band failing proposal lands at low ("Trivial (= the zero-interaction band)", S06.4). (4) Critique re-runs fresh after a raising TIER-UP (re-enters "at the new tier's requirements"); a no-op TIER-UP (already at/above the proposed tier) proceeds with the verdict attached — structurally loop-free; exhausted REVISE rounds proceed with findings attached (⚙ help text: "ships as-is with the critique attached"). (5) Requester-contested Re-plan revisions re-run the spine, not a second paid critique pass (S06.10 ceremony economics: at most two heavyweight passes; Stage-2 floor re-check is the deterministic guard). (6) Coverage decision-card choices are replan-once-more / drop (recorded; pre-approval renumber keeps SetObjective's contiguous 1..n law, prior versions preserve traceability) / proceed-with-gap-listed; SPEC-DOUBT's "rethink" cancels the intake (S06.8 card verbs not enumerated by the spec). (7) Pre-approval intake cards are all requester-answered (D10 explicitly for approval; single-user v0 posture); High-tier PIN step-up rides the §9 approvals-family call at the API layer (B2-4/B6), not here. (8) Intake writes no S02.4 checkpoint rows — stage boundaries are D7 checkpoints via durable artifacts + state events + ledger writes; checkpoint-per-paid-call rows land through the adapter Driver when stage sessions become real engine runs (B2-4). (9) Cancel maps parked→`finalized` (finalize-with-card) and running→`completed` with the cancel reason (S02.3 has no cancelled terminal).

## 15. Verification conventions (P3-B2-3; S07, S02.7)

- **Package map:** `internal/verify` is the S07 pipeline — V0 pre-gates (`v0.go`), V1 domain check packs + the did-research check (`v1.go`), V2 dual-axis judge (`v2.go`), bounded rework (`rework.go`), typed escalation (`escalate.go`), entailment machinery idle (`entailment.go`), S07.11 recording (`record.go`), the drain (`pipeline.go`), seed artifacts (`seeds.go`). Imports: storage/eventlog/ledger/intake/adapters — **NEVER `internal/gates`**: the quality gate is structurally separate from the effects gate (S07.1/S02.7), enforced by a conformance test on the package import graph plus a SHIP-releases-nothing e2e over a live journal row. Verdict recording touches no effect-journal row; the D7 sequence stays quality verdict → V3 human accept → gated effect execution.
- **Event types** `verify.{v0,v1,round,escalation}` are provisional pending the S14 event contract (B5), extending §7/§8/§13/§14's note. `verify.round` is the per-round verdict record — **keep-forever class (G2 Def.11)**, noted on the payload (`retention`) and honest about judge calibration (`golden_set.measured=false` until the B4 run). All verify appends are control-plane acts at the run's current generation, owner-attributed (15.6).
- **Escalation (S07.7):** the route table is total over the 8 categories; the only sinks are decision card / operator alert / worker-flaw ticket; cards are durable `asks` rows (`ask-verify-`/`canary-` id prefixes) written in one tx with their `verify.escalation` event. SLA ⚙ keys are consumed at card issue (approval: remind/push hours; safety: immediate + re-ping). The worker-flaw ticket seam is stubbed to an inbox-visible ask until S08 lands (B3). The planted-defect e2e class (one test per category) is a **standing conformance-suite entry**; canary task shape + silence watch and the drill cadence helper live here — **their scheduling/alert wiring is S14's (B5)**. CHECK-INTEGRITY findings never enter the REVISE drain: suite defects route to card + quarantine (regenerating the deliverable cannot fix a broken check); the mechanical fact stands and V3 still gates.
- **V1 runner:** `CheckRunner` seam; production `SandboxCheckRunner` composes via `adapters.Confiner` at class **C2** (writable workspace for build/test rungs) — network-off TODAY because egress classes ride an empty netns until the host egress substrate lands (§12); **when that substrate lands the verification profile MUST stay network-off (P-T06-2) — flagged B4 obligation**. Pass/fail derives platform-side from the wait status, never in-band (S07.3 rule 3). The verification workspace (VCS-history-stripped revision) is the S13 seam (B4); dev callers hand a prepared dir.
- **Non-⚙ constants** (S18 ratifies no key — the §7/§9/§11/§14 precedent, under the standing settings-tab directive): `v0RegenerationLimit = 1` (S07.2 ratified "one regeneration"), `convergenceSimilarity = 0.9` (the S07.6 cosmetic-edit bar over line-set Jaccard), the launch-domain roster `{software}` (S07.8/S19 rollout structure), the `PlaceholderMarkers` lexicon (exported, conservative), the default check class `"C2"`.
- **Seeds (S07.10/S07.4):** software rubric bundle + software golden set (26 cases: 20 planted across the defect classes + 6 clean controls) + entailment calibration set (10 planted pairs) ship in code (`SeedSoftwareRubric`/`SeedGoldenSet`/`SeedCalibrationSet`), versioned, operator-editable via strict-JSON `Load*` (unknown fields fail loudly) with `WriteSeed` for gate review — the §14 taxonomy precedent; formal 8.3-gate entry at B3 (S09). **Seed content ratification = B2 gate item; TPR/TNR measurement = B4 local tier** (deferral record `P3/measurements/2026-07-20-verification-seeds-deferral.md`). The rubric pins the judge as the ratified planning-model CLASS; the concrete engine pin lands with S08 selection (B3) as a version bump gated on a golden-set re-run (P-T06-5).
- **Judge seam (S07.5):** `Compliance` and `Sanity` are separate prompted calls plus `Meta()` (model + self-family flag, always recorded); the input slice is built ONLY through the ledger's clean-context `Assemble` (`Clean: true`) with the S07-owned Extra items (artifact/diff/rubric/V1-outcomes/prior-findings) — there is no transcript field anywhere. Platform validation: missing AC verdicts force Unknown; a PASS without an extractive (substring) evidence quote forces Unknown; V1-executed criteria take the mechanical outcome (disagreement → CHECK-INTEGRITY, never override); an uncited blocker is demoted to note with a recorded flag; after round 1 new note keys are suppressed to a count.

## 16. S07.7 resume-in-place — answering non-intake asks (P3-B2-5; S07.6–S07.7, S02.3/S02.5, S06.9)

- **Park-on-card:** a verify run whose drain terminates in a card PARKS on the durable ask (S02.3: parked = "suspended on a limit event or an open gate/ask"; S07.7: escalations are "structured, resumable asks; answering resumes the pipeline in place") — the B2-4 completed-on-card shape is superseded. ALL drain-terminal categories park (one uniform resume substrate); at B2-5 only the rework-family cards are answerable — CAP-HIT / AC-BLOCKER / SANITY-BLOCKER, exactly the cards issued with the three verbs — and every other category's answer rejects loudly (`ErrUnsupportedAnswer`) until its verbs' packet lands, never silently absorbed. Parked-on-gate stays blocked-not-failed (§8: `stale_finalize` never applies).
- **Answer surface:** verify asks route by the §15 id prefixes (`ask-verify-`/`canary-`) through the SAME answer endpoint as intake cards (`Surface.Answer` → `Skeleton.AnswerVerifyAsk`); verb/quality semantics live in `internal/verify/answer.go`, run-FSM choreography in `internal/stage/answer.go`. Ask close + run transition commit in ONE WriteTx (the intake `closeAndResume` shape); the human decision entry records first (the ledger opens its own tx and never nests, §14). D10: the ask row's owner answers. S01.9 High-tier step-up stays scoped to the intake approvals family — a verify decision card releases nothing (effects gate untouched, S07.1) and V3 accept mechanics with their own gating are S13's (B4).
- **The three verbs (S07.7):** `accept_best_effort` resumes through THE ratified resume edge (parked→running, generation bump — kept uniform across verbs so the fence holds for all of them) then completes in the same tx; the quality record stays honest — no SHIP verdict is minted, work items stay `done_unverified` (`SetVerified` remains the only path to verified, S05.1/S07.11), the acceptance is a human decision entry. `revise_with_guidance` resumes and re-enters the drain INLINE in the answer request (the B2-4 answer-drives-stages pattern). `cancel` is §14 reading 9 verbatim: parked→finalized, finalize-with-card — no resume edge, generation unchanged; kanban `cancelled`. Terminal answers settle the run explicitly (receipts per run-end; idempotent with the dispatch path's auto-settle).
- **Resumed drain (S07.6):** guidance becomes numbered blocker findings entering the RETRY PACKAGE via the ratified requester-comment channel ("requester comments enter the retry through the same numbered-anchored-point channel"); it is never re-merged as post-rework round findings — the guidance described the PRE-rework artifact, and re-merging would force REVISE unconditionally — it rides the judge's prior-findings scope instead. Round NUMBERING continues across the carried history (one coherent durable record; a recurrent stop re-parks with the FULL history), while the ⚙ `verification.rework_rounds` cap counts each drain invocation's judged rounds afresh (an answer is an explicit human grant of a fresh bounded budget — the S06.7(a) requester-granted-round precedent); note-suppression keys and the convergence state seed from the carried history, so cross-park model inertia still trips the stop.
- **The best-effort pin is durable:** every rework revision persists to the artifact store (stage `engineRevise`), and round records carry `(revision, content_sha256)` — a card's pin always denotes retrievable content. The resume path loads the pinned revision back and hash-verifies it, refusing to rework unpinned or drifted content; cards from pre-B2-5 builds (completed runs, no pin) reject `ErrNotResumable` with the reason.
- **Fencing & recovery:** parked→running bumps the generation (§8); superseded stage sessions (asserted spawn-time generation, §13) are rejected by the event-log fence — proven on the answer path by the standing e2e battery. A resumed drain that errors mid-flight crashes the run for the recovery ladder (the dispatch-leg posture); the answered ask stays closed and a recovered lineage re-verifies into its own card — no finding dies silently (S07.7). Engine sessions of the verify leg ride the deliverable's/brief's OWN run id, never a suffix-composed one (a fork-suffixed run would otherwise session against its superseded parent).

## 17. Memory & knowledge conventions (P3-B3-1; S09, S05.4, S02.2)

- **Package map:** `internal/memory` is the S09 subsystem — `Store` (reads, the server-scoped FTS5 `knowledge_search`, the S2.9 influence join), `Gate` (the S09.4 station-3 human write path: CreateManual/Import/NewVersion/Adopt/Remove/TrueDelete/ResolveConflict), `Source` (the S05.4 Knowledge seam), `EnsureB2SeedGovernance` (S09.10). Migration `0004_knowledge.sql`: `knowledge_entries` + `lesson_proposals` + `knowledge_adoptions` (S09.2, field lists final), the `knowledge_conflicts` edge table (S09.7 — the question card's durable home; asks rows require a run, gate-time writes have none), and `knowledge_fts` (FTS5 external-content over ENTRIES ONLY — proposals are structurally outside every search/assembly path, S09.2). FTS5 availability at the pinned driver is proven by a standing test, never assumed.
- **Capability withholding (S09.1), the realized shape:** writes live on `*Gate`, reads on `*Store` (compile-time split); engine-facing packages (`adapters`, `adapters/claudecli`, `stage`, `ledger`) never import `memory` — pinned by a standing AST conformance test (the §15 import-graph precedent) — the composition root hands stage only the `ledger.Source` view; gate actors must hold a `users` row (reserved ids and the §9 dev identity are structurally refused), house scope demands the operator role (D10); the `observation` kind is refused everywhere — L1 dormancy IS the absence of any writer (S09.4); migration triggers abort in-place content mutation, identity changes, and row deletion even for raw-SQL holders (edit = new version row, purge = tombstone). `knowledge_search`: adapter-injected run id → owner + project derived server-side; free text is sanitized to quoted AND-joined FTS5 terms (operators/column filters cannot ride a query); zero-hit searches append `knowledge.search_miss` (the S09.3 retrieval-miss record the ⚙ vector-gate triggers will evaluate post-15.3).
- **Injection (S05.4 × S09.8):** the Source selects house/project/user by conservative selector matching — a selector demanding a fact the platform cannot confirm does not match (domain/task-type/trigger facts arrive with the registry, B4; the project fact = `artifact_claims` until then, the §14 safe direction); per-scope ⚙ `memory.injection_budget_tokens.*` metered by the structural bytes/4 estimate; the ratified drop order lowest-specificity → oldest-`last_injected_at` → entry id; drops ride the manifest as `over_budget_dropped` (`ManifestEntry.Disposition`; blocks never) plus a level-triggered curation ask `ask-knowledge-curation-<scope>-<owner>-<nanos>` (one open per scope+owner; house cards address the first operator); open conflict pairs entering one frame get `ManifestEntry.ConflictsWith` on both sides and a re-raise `knowledge.conflict` event. `ledger.SliceQuery` gained `RunID` — resolved by Assemble itself, used for source-side bookkeeping acts only, never as a selection key. Wiring: planner sessions + execute stages (the sites whose comments named the B3 seam); the judge's clean slice stays the S07-fixed set (§15) and the critic stays artifact-only (§14).
- **Events:** `knowledge.{write,remove,delete,conflict,curation}` platform-scope + `knowledge.search_miss` run-scoped, refs-not-blobs (content rides as sha256) — provisional pending the S14 event contract (B5), extending §7/§8/§13–§16's note.
- **Named seams (stubbed, never invented inline):** `memory.Committer` — the S13 git commit-on-approval with the approver as author (D9); nil leaves `file_commit` NULL, and the migration trigger permits exactly the one NULL→hash fill when B4 lands. `memory.ContradictionScreen` — the S09.7 advisory local-model duty (S12, B4); nil skips the advisory pass, the deterministic same-`topic_key` detection runs regardless and is never suppressed by the screen.
- **S09.9 containment (closes TBD-P3(auto-memory containment) per the B1-4 measurement):** `claudecli` wipes the contents of `$CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR/memory/` at every session START, fail-closed, before the engine process exists — auth siblings untouched (the measurement's redirect constraint), Resume exempt (same run continuing its own L0 scratch), empty cred ref exempt (compiled-config channel not engaged; bare dev spawns only). Cross-user isolation was already structural (per-user config roots); this closes the cross-RUN channel: no session ever starts with engine-carried memory outside the platform record. Engine per-project memory (`projects/<slug>/`) is task-scoped by the isolated per-run cwd already; exit-time deletion composes with workspace GC when S02.10 machinery lands. The per-pin canary re-checks the write root on every engine bump (P-T10-1).
- **Seed governance (S09.10):** shell boot runs `EnsureB2SeedGovernance` — the B2-ratified seed objects (2 taxonomies, P47 triggers, software rubric, golden set, calibration set) become six governed house-scope entries with stable `seed-*` ids, origin `imported`, provenance = the B2-gate ratification (`P3/gates/B2-report.md §7 D3`, 2026-07-20), file-backed under `knowledge/house/` as the SAME strict JSON the owning packages' `Load*` paths parse (proven by test — the governed file IS the operator-editable override input). Idempotent; a removed/tombstoned object is never resurrected; skipped with a log until an operator account exists (house needs its D10 holder). The in-code seeds remain the runtime source for intake/verify at v0 (S06.3 "v0 changes = manual operator edits"); consumer rewiring onto the store is not S09's to do.
- **Clearly-implied readings (section-cited):** (1) golden/calibration sets carry kind `exemplar` — the S09.10 verification row's "reference exemplars" class; the kind enum is final and names no eval-fixture kind. (2) S09.2's `owner_user` is realized as the `user_id` column (names `[coordinator-draft]`; the 0001 attribution convention wins). (3) Project-owner approval (S09.4 station 3) narrows to the registry's project owner at B4; until then a project write is owner-attributed to its actor. (4) Removal is owner-or-operator; TRUE deletion is strictly the owner (S4.1 phrases it as the owner's right). (5) Supersession retires the old version in the same tx (S09.8 "superseded versions retired") and skips conflict edges against the entry it supersedes. (6) `Numbers()` is the one dotted-key read surface for the six lifecycle/pipeline ⚙ values + the two vector-gate triggers; dormant-station machinery consumes it at v1 activation — no station logic shipped (S09.4 v0 column).

## 18. Worker store, guardrail split & composer-battery conventions (P3-B3-2; S08.1–S08.7, S08.9–S08.10, S03.5, S09, S11.6)

- **Package map:** `internal/worker` is the S08 store/compile/battery substrate — template file format + strict frontmatter parser (`template.go`, `yamlfm.go`), static-only skills (`skill.go`), station 1 (`lint.go`), station 2 + the v0 ceiling table (`ceiling.go`), the per-invocation compile (`compile.go`), rows+files store verbs (`store.go`, `lifecycle.go`), stations 3–4 + validation records (`battery.go`). `internal/worker/automation` is the S08.9 dialect (parser + on-demand executor); a standing AST conformance test pins that it never imports `internal/adapters`/`internal/stage` — **no model in the loop is an import-graph fact**, extending §15/§17. `internal/worker` itself **never imports `internal/memory`** (also AST-pinned): the overlay slice rides the `worker.OverlaySource` seam, satisfied at the composition root by `memory.Store.OverlaySlice` (new, S08.4×S09.2) — the S09.1 capability walls stay transitively clean for the engine-facing wiring B3-3 adds. **S08.8 routing/selection is NOT here** (B3-3); named seams left for it: `DryEngine` (production = dispatch on the requester's lane), `CompileForRun` (the dispatcher's per-spawn call; records `ConfigHash` on the run), gap-proposal card surface (`RecordGap` returns proposal-due), `ledger.Sources.Worker` (untouched), `stage.DefaultModel` + window constant (untouched, retire at B3-3).
- **Migration 0005** (S08.1 field lists faithful): `domains` (day-one rows: software=full rubric `seed-verify-rubric-software`; web-research=degraded — full at v0.1), `worker_templates` (UNIQUE(owner,name); `active_version` the only mutable pointer), `worker_template_versions` (immutable; fill-once approved/graduated/file_commit — the §17 NULL→hash pattern for the B4 committer), `worker_guardrails` (mutable ONLY first_n_remaining, downward), `validation_records` (append-only; approver fill-once; revalidation = NEW row), `gap_records` (occurrences never decrease). All walls are triggers, proven by a raw-SQL attempted-violation battery.
- **Guardrail split realization (S08.2/S08.6):** the file may request EQUIPMENT only; class/egress/budgets/gate-policy requests ride `requested_grants` on the VERSION ROW (control-plane, never file). Guardrail-field detection is normalized whole-key matching (exact set + family prefixes; never substrings — `task_classes` can't trip) over every frontmatter key path of template AND skill files, plus `key:`-line screening of overlay content; conservative-toward-reject on the enforcement channel. A guardrail field rejects at CreateDraft, at station 1, at station 2 (restated), and at compile (steered-overlay belt). Grants land in `worker_guardrails` exactly once, at Approve.
- **Template file format (S08.1):** markdown + a STRICT Sinet-owned YAML subset (two levels; flow lists; no tabs/anchors/flow-maps; dup keys reject) — stdlib-first, no YAML adoption; the store persists the CANONICAL render (parse∘render fixed point; comments are not content), which is what is hashed, diffed, and (B4) committed. **Persona is structured frontmatter** (`persona:` list) so the ⚙ `workers.persona_lines_max` warn is mechanically checkable (S08.5's "at most ⚙ lines" needs a countable channel). Skill static-only = closed SKILL.md frontmatter allowlist (no hook/command slot exists) + no exec bits + no symlinks, walked at load.
- **Battery conventions (S08.6):** green = station-1 no errors AND station-2 structurally admissible AND a completed witnessed run; **above-ceiling flags do NOT redden validation — they gate approval**: `Approve` refuses unacknowledged flagged items (`ErrAboveCeiling`; `AckFlagged` is the explicit, event-recorded override), while a Rule-of-Two three-property combination is a HARD station-2 refusal (the S08.6 sentence contrast: "flagged line" vs "structural reject"). Props derivation (recorded on the audit): untrusted-input = any egress reach or automation payloads; sensitive = connectors (broker creds); state/external = C2-write, egress, or automation; **standing supervision = the automation proposal path only** — first-N and degraded-domain review are temporary and deliberately don't admit a 3-prop agentic worker. Station 3: agentic = compile-the-draft (requested-as-granted; the witness runs the real config) at C1-or-capped-C2 with `CeilingCostUSD` = ⚙ `workers.dryrun_cost_cap_usd` through the `DryEngine` seam (tests fake it — zero paid calls); automation = sample-payload execution, outward steps journaling S02.7 proposals (free, D7). Dry-run pair → `GoldenSeed{verified:false}` on the record (R15-OQ4 flow). Records key (version × model × engine-pin); automations put `automation.DialectVersion` in the pin slot with model empty.
- **Lifecycle readings (section-cited):** kind and domain are fixed at creation — a new domain is a new template (S08.1: the domain ref drives 7.6 marking; S08.4 versions are edits of ONE definition). First-N: seeded from ⚙ at approval; carried from the predecessor's live counter when the diff touches neither body nor equipment, else reset (G3 D3.4); count-based decrements via `RecordSupervisedReview`; graduation stamps the version. A FLAGGED worker re-enters active service by **revalidate (new dated record) + Repoint** — approval and guardrails stand; there is no second approval of the same version (one guardrails row per version). `Delivery` is the S08.7/S08.10 structural read: degraded domain ⇒ requires review (never unsupervised while degraded), flagged ⇒ supervised-only, first-N>0 ⇒ review. `CreateDomain` (operator, D10) births domains DEGRADED (2.1 honesty); degraded-domain `schedule_attachable` grants are allowed — the auto-accept refusal is the v1 schedule layer's check against `Delivery` (S08.7 bans auto-accept schedules, not reviewed ones). Unresolved knowledge topic keys are station-1 WARNINGS (injection is conservative-match, S05.4/S09; the dry run witnesses under-equipment), unresolved tools/skills are errors.
- **Compile (S08.3×S03.5):** `CompileInvocation` is pure, per-invocation; output feeds `adapters.StartRequest` verbatim (AgentsJSON in the spike shape `{name:{description,prompt}}`, tool allowlist, gated tools, permission mode, SessionStart path, class, ceilings). `ConfigHash` = sha256 over canonical JSON of file-hash + guardrails + overlay (ids/versions/content-hashes) + instance refs + every compiled field + skill refs — body+config one unit; instance refs make it per-run. Task*-family grants are refused at compile AND at lowering (belt+adapter). The instance's L0 ledger section reaches the engine through the S05 stage brief, not the agent definition (S08.3: brief assembly is S05's). **Skills compile as resolved dirs + content hashes** (`Compiled.SkillDirs`); engine-side pointing is a named B3-3 seam — the S03.5 skill channel is closed (`--disable-slash-commands`) and no pointing knob exists in the current lowering, so placement rides the dispatch wiring, never a discovery scan.
- **⚙ discipline:** exactly the 4 declared `workers.*` keys consumed, by dotted key: `first_n` (Approve seed + card), `gap_proposal_count` (`RecordGap` compose-when-earned threshold), `persona_lines_max` (station-1 warn), `dryrun_cost_cap_usd` (station-3 ceiling). Events `worker.{template_created,version_created,validated,approved,promoted,repointed,flagged,retired,review,graduated,gap,domain_maturity,automation_run}` are platform-scope, owner-attributed, provisional pending S14 (extending §7/§8/§13–§17's note).
- **S09 boundary held, no conflict:** worker-overlay writes stay refused at v0 (`memory.ErrScopeDormant` untouched); the compile requests the slice through the seam and receives structurally-empty — S08.4's injection contract is day-one machinery whose content arrives at v1 activation. Tests simulate the v1 writer by raw row insert (the schema ships whole; dormancy is the absence of a writer, S09.4) and prove labeled most-specific-last concatenation + per-user disjoint instances (10.1).

## 19. Routing & S04 spawn/helper conventions (P3-B3-3; S08.8, S04, S03.2, S12 seams)
